#=============================================================================
# Simplest area synthesis for the shared-FMA thesis (Synopsys Design Compiler)
#
# Goal: show that the ADDED hardware (arbiter + accelerator FSM) is far smaller
#       than the AVOIDED hardware (a second FMA / fp_wrapper) -> "no second FPU".
#
# Run from the x-heep root, once per TOP:
#     dc_shell -f dc/synth_area.tcl | tee dc/reports/log_<TOP>.txt
#
# Only 3 things to set below: LIB_DB (your standard-cell .db), TOP, CLK_NS.
#=============================================================================
# ----------------------------- USER SETTINGS --------------------------------
set XHEEP   [pwd]                                ;# x-heep root (run DC from here)
/2tb/home/oyurtsever/x-heep
set LIB_DB  "./dc/sc12mc_cln40g_base_rvt_c40_ss_typical_max_0p81v_125c.db"         ;# <-- your standard-cell library (.db)
./dc/sc12mc_cln40g_base_rvt_c40_ss_typical_max_0p81v_125c.db
set TOP     "cv32e40px_top"                      ;# cv32e40px_top | dma_fp_dot_accel
cv32e40px_top
set CLK_NS  10.0                                 ;# clock period ns (10 = 100 MHz)
10.0
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Per-TOP: filelist, macro defines, clock port
# Defaults (used by the standalone-module runs, which are the faithful numbers).
set FLIST   "dc/rtl_core.f"    ;# analyze everything, elaborate only $TOP's subtree
dc/rtl_core.f
set DEFINES {}
set PARAMS  ""
set CLKPORT "clk_i"
clk_i
switch $TOP {
    dma_fp_dot_accel {
        # the accelerator FSM we ADD  (all ports are top-level -> nothing optimised away)
        set FLIST "dc/rtl_accel.f"
    }
    cv32e40px_fp_wrapper {
        # the FMA we AVOID duplicating (a second FPU). Standalone -> true FMA area.
    }
    dma_apu_arbiter {
        # the CPU-priority arbiter we ADD. Standalone -> true arbiter area.
    }
    cv32e40px_top {
        # Optional whole-core context. MUST force FPU=1, else the `if (FPU) fpu_gen`
        # block (which holds BOTH the FMA and our arbiter) is dropped. COPROC_FPU_SHARE
        # embeds the arbiter. NOTE: if the core does not drive the FPU, DC may optimise
        # parts of it away -> prefer the standalone runs above for the actual areas.
        set DEFINES {COPROC_FPU_SHARE}
        set PARAMS  "FPU=1"
    }
}
FPU=1
# ------------------------------- library ------------------------------------
set target_library $LIB_DB
./dc/sc12mc_cln40g_base_rvt_c40_ss_typical_max_0p81v_125c.db
set link_library   [concat "*" $LIB_DB]
* ./dc/sc12mc_cln40g_base_rvt_c40_ss_typical_max_0p81v_125c.db
define_design_lib WORK -path ./dc/work
1
# include dirs (for `include resolution inside common_cells / fpnew)
set search_path [concat $search_path \
    $XHEEP/hw/vendor/pulp_platform/common_cells/include \
    $XHEEP/hw/vendor/pulp_platform/register_interface/include ]
. /eda/synopsys/syn/S-2021.06-SP5/libraries/syn /eda/synopsys/syn/S-2021.06-SP5/dw/syn_ver /eda/synopsys/syn/S-2021.06-SP5/dw/sim_ver /2tb/home/oyurtsever/x-heep/hw/vendor/pulp_platform/common_cells/include /2tb/home/oyurtsever/x-heep/hw/vendor/pulp_platform/register_interface/include
# ------------------------------ read RTL ------------------------------------
set fh [open $XHEEP/$FLIST r]
file14
set files {}
foreach line [split [read $fh] "\n"] {
    set line [string trim $line]
    if {$line ne "" && ![string match "#*" $line]} { lappend files $XHEEP/$line }
}
close $fh
if {[llength $DEFINES]} {
    analyze -format sverilog -define $DEFINES $files
} else {
    analyze -format sverilog $files
}
